<script>
        (function() {
          function isValidEmail(str) {
            
            var emailRegex = /^(([^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+(\.[^<>()[\]\\.,;:\s@\"]+)*)|(\".+\"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
            return str && str.length < 256 && emailRegex.test(str)
          }

          
          
          
          var parsedQueryParams = document.location.search
            .replace(/^\?/, '')
            .split(/[&;]/g)
            .filter(function(param) {return param})
            .map(function(param) {
                
                var parts = param.replace(/\+/g, ' ').split('=')
                var key = parts.shift()
                var value = parts.length > 0 ? parts.join('=') : undefined
                try {
                  key = decodeURIComponent(key);
                } catch (e) {}
                try {
                  value = value === undefined ? null : decodeURIComponent(value);
                } catch(e) {}
                return [key, value]
            })

          
          var paramLookup = parsedQueryParams
            .reduce(function(obj, param) {
              obj[param[0]] = param[1]
              return obj
            },{})

          

          
          
          if (paramLookup['cm_email'] && isValidEmail(paramLookup['cm_email'])) {
            document.cookie = 'cm_email=' + paramLookup['cm_email'] + ';path=/'
          }

          
          var hiddenParams = [
            'cm_email',
            'customer_email',
            'customer_name',
            
            'email',
            'question_requester_email',
            'question_requester_name'
          ]

          
          
          
          var replacementQuerystring = parsedQueryParams
            .filter(function(param) {return param[0] && hiddenParams.indexOf(param[0]) === -1})
            .map(function(param) {return (encodeURIComponent(param[0]) + '=' + encodeURIComponent(param[1])).replace(/%20/g, '+')})
            .join('&')

          
          replacementQuerystring = replacementQuerystring ? ('?' + replacementQuerystring ) : ''

          
          if (document.location.search !== replacementQuerystring) {
            try {
              var baseUrl = [location.protocol, '//', location.host, location.pathname].join('')
              window.history.replaceState({}, '', baseUrl + replacementQuerystring)
            } catch (e) {
              
              document.location.search = replacementQuerystring
          }
        }
        })()
    </script>

        function getCookieByName(name) {
            var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
            for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                var c = ca[i];
                while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1);

  window.onStatus = function onStatus(status) {
        if (status !== 'online') {
            zenchatPlaceholderLabel.innerHTML = 'Currently unavailable';
            window.ga && window.ga('send', 'event', 'Live Chat', 'Offline', {nonInteraction:1});

                sessionStorage.setItem('hideZenchat', true);
